Abstract: We study scalar and electromagnetic perturbations of the root--Kerr object. Its source-free exterior carries the electromagnetic field obtained in the vanishing-mass limit of the Kerr--Newman black hole, while its physical source is a rotating disk with an essential distributional rim contribution. For a massless charged scalar, the minimally coupled Klein--Gordon equation separates into a confluent-Heun (CHE) radial equation with a direct coupling between the scalar and background charges. We give the associated Nekrasov--Shatashvili (NS) dictionary and show that the disk is an ordinary radial point. For electromagnetic pertubations, we derive the equations for the radiative Newman--Penrose Maxwell scalars directly. They have the same confluent-Heun structure, but a Maxwell perturbation has no bulk coupling to the background potential. Charge-dependent photon scattering data therefore cannot be fixed by the exterior equation alone. We formulate the disk/rim boundary-data problem, identify the response data required to convert exact CHE/NS connection coefficients into a physical scattering matrix, and show how all-spin helicity-flip Compton information constrains the response on the radiative subspace. The analysis separates exact exterior propagation and amplitude matching from the additional source dynamics required for a microscopic response.

</details>

Abstract: We study circular geodesics of neutral test particles in the static charged solution of Scalar--Tensor--Vector Gravity (STVG), and we use the twin kilohertz quasiperiodic oscillations (QPOs) of twelve accreting compact objects to bound its parameters. Starting from the effective potential we obtain closed forms for the specific energy, the specific angular momentum and the Keplerian angular velocity, and from these the radial and vertical epicyclic frequencies. The horizon, photon sphere, shadow radius and innermost stable circular orbit are given in closed form as well, and each one reduces to the RN and Schwarzschild value in the appropriate limit. Null geodesics are integrated numerically and show how the enhanced coupling widens the capture cross section while leaving the logarithmic divergence of the deflection angle at the photon sphere intact. Within the RP model we then run Metropolis--Hastings MCMC simulations on the QPO pairs of eight neutron stars and four microquasars, comparing the Schwarzschild spacetime, the RN spacetime, STVG with vanishing charge and the full STVG solution. The best fits are obtained mainly for the charged solutions in the neutron star sample, while all four models describe the black hole sample equally well. We show that the orbital dynamics depends on the mass $M$, the coupling $α$ and the charge $Q$ only through the two combinations $\Meff=(1+α)M$ and $\Qeff^{2}=(1+α)(αM^{2}+Q^{2})$, which accounts for the multimodal posteriors found in the neutron star sample and sets a model-independent limit on what QPO timing alone can measure. The astrophysical implications of these bounds, in particular for inferred masses above the Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff limit, are discussed in detail.

Abstract: We propose a single-field mechanism that dynamically prepares the initial conditions required for exponential plateau inflation. The inflaton is decomposed into a coarse-grained mode and a high-occupancy, approximately Gaussian band of relativistic nonzero modes. Gaussian--Hartree averaging of the potential produces an exact variance-dependent minimum, which places the coarse-grained field high on the plateau when the excitation variance is large. Under a translation-invariant microcanonical measure, the available phase-space volume is exponentially concentrated near this minimum, while an order-one homogeneous kinetic-energy fraction is exponentially atypical. During expansion, the preparation modes redshift as radiation, $σ^2\propto a^{-2}$ and $ρ_χ\propto a^{-4}$. Although the induced minimum rapidly moves toward the vacuum, the force acting on the coarse-grained mode is bounded by an exponentially small factor, yielding analytic upper limits on its velocity, displacement, and kinetic fraction. We derive a sufficient condition for the subsequent number of slow-roll e-folds and exhibit a benchmark with more than 60 e-folds. The mechanism also motivates correlated finite-onset signatures: large-scale power suppression with decaying oscillations and a variance-convolution bispectrum.

Abstract: The McVittie metric describes a self-gravitating compact object embedded in an expanding universe. It inherits two singularities: a curvature singularity at $r \to 0$ and a cosmological singularity at $a(t) \to 0$. Motivated by the construction of black-bounce geometries, we regularize both of these singularities and propose a regularized McVittie metric. The central singularity is regularized by replacing $r \to \sqrt{r^{2}+b^{2}}$, leading to a geometry that interpolates between a cosmological black hole, a black bounce, and a traversable wormhole. Similarly, the cosmological singularity is regularized by introducing a non-vanishing scale factor $a \rightarrow \sqrt{a^{2} + a_b^{2}}$. The resulting spacetime is supported by an effective imperfect fluid with finite anisotropic stress. We investigate the formation of the apparent horizon, analyze the circular geodesic structure, and argue that the geometry can also be interpreted as a conformally evolving Morris-Thorne wormhole embedded in a regular cosmological background.

*Shahnawaz A. Adil,Özgür Akarsu,Mariam Bouhmadi-López,Beñat Ibarra-Uriondo,Nihan Katırcı,J. Alberto Vázquez*

TL;DR: 本文研究了三种暗能量模型(ECDM、SSCDM和LΛ CDM)的标量场重构问题,发现现象学上相似的暗能量演化历史可能没有可比的标量场实现。研究揭示了不同模型在场映射存在性、可逆性和端点正则性方面的根本差异,特别指出紧致SSCDM模型无法通过具有局部Lipschitz力的势函数精确重现其冻结平台特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现象学暗能量模型通常根据观测数据拟合演化历史,但这些历史能否对应合理的标量场动力学实现尚不清楚。特别是对于具有负-正密度符号切换的暗能量模型,需要明确其标量场重构的可行性、正则性和唯一性问题。研究者希望通过具体分析三种代表性模型,评估从现象学历史到标量场理论的映射是否存在以及存在何种数学病理。

Method: 采用固定动能符号的最小耦合标量场重构方法,针对三种预设的暗能量演化历史(误差函数模型ECDM、紧致光滑阶跃模型SSCDM和阶梯模型LΛ CDM)进行标量场反演。通过分析连续的负-正密度交叉选择幽灵分支,检验重构势函数的正则性、端点行为和Lipschitz性质。进行条件合成比较,使用sigmoid-高斯族和广义类轴子族函数拟合,并执行闭合测试验证未调参演化与目标历史的一致性。分析场映射的存在性、可逆性和端点正则性作为可实现性判据。

Result: ECDM模型在每个有限红移处产生光滑的在壳势,其状态方程极点仅是比值奇点;sigmoid-高斯族对其拟合效果最优,闭合测试中最大偏差约0.16。精确SSCDM具有正则轨迹但端点势为C¹非C²型,满足V-Vₑ∝|φ-φₑ|^(4/3),其非Lipschitz力允许从冻结平台延迟离开,导致重构势和平台数据无法唯一生成预定历史;闭合测试结构性失败,无法用局部Lipschitz力的势函数从冻结初始数据精确重现有限持续时间的冻结平台。精确阶梯模型需要分布型动能应力,在所采用的单场作用量下没有普通经典实现。代表性正演解展示负-正标量密度交叉,势零点先于密度零点出现。

Conclusion: 现象学上相似的符号切换暗能量历史并不必然具有可比的标量场实现。在将现象学历史解释为标量动力学之前,必须评估场映射的存在性、可逆性和端点正则性。特别是紧致SSCDM模型存在根本性障碍,无法通过常规标量场理论精确实现其特征性冻结平台结构。研究表明幽灵作用量仅作为均匀有效代理使用,不同暗能量模型的标量场可实现性存在本质差异,这对暗能量理论建模具有重要约束意义。

Abstract: Phenomenologically similar sign-switching dark-energy histories need not have comparable scalar-field realizations. We reconstruct minimally coupled scalars with fixed kinetic sign for three prescribed histories: the error-function model (ECDM), the compact smooth-step model (SSCDM), and the ladder-like model (L$Λ$CDM). Continuous negative-to-positive density crossings select the phantom branch. ECDM yields a smooth on-shell potential at every finite redshift; its equation-of-state pole at the density zero is only a ratio singularity. Exact SSCDM has a regular trajectory but a $C^1$, non-$C^2$ endpoint potential, $V-V_e\propto|φ-φ_e|^{4/3}$. Its non-Lipschitz force permits delayed departures from frozen plateaus, so the reconstructed potential and plateau data do not uniquely generate the prescribed history. The exact Ladder requires distributional kinetic stress and has no ordinary classical realization in the adopted one-field action. In conditional synthetic comparisons, the sigmoid--Gaussian family ranks highest for ECDM and the generalized axion-like family for SSCDM, although the fitted axion exponents $n<1/2$ imply a divergent endpoint force. Representative regular forward solutions exhibit negative-to-positive scalar-density crossings, with the potential zero preceding the density zero. In a closure test, unretuned evolution of the top-ranked ECDM template tracks the target approximately, $\max|Δ\widetildeΩ_φ|\simeq0.16$. Closure fails structurally for compact SSCDM: no potential with a locally Lipschitz force can reproduce its exact finite-duration frozen plateaus from exactly frozen initial data. Field-map existence, invertibility, and endpoint regularity must be assessed before interpreting a phenomenological history as scalar dynamics. The phantom action is used only as a homogeneous effective proxy.

</details>

Result: 研究发现,在捕获视界形成后,壳层与真空态负频模式之间的相互作用会不可避免地立即发生,这是半经典引力坍缩的普适特征。这种相互作用导致能量从坍缩物质转移到向外传播的霍金辐射,产生"引力量子摩擦"效应。该效应一方面阻碍捕获区域的形成,另一方面将坍缩物质携带的信息印刻到辐射量子上,实现了信息的转移而非丢失。

Conclusion: 引力量子摩擦机制为黑洞信息丢失问题提供了新的解决方案:在半经典引力框架内,无需额外物理假设,物质与几何的普适耦合就能自然产生信息保存机制。该机制可能改变坍缩的最终结果,潜在地阻止视界和奇点的形成。这表明半经典引力理论本身可能已经包含了解决信息悖论的关键要素,为进一步理解量子引力和黑洞物理提供了重要理论启示。

Abstract: We study the gravitational collapse of a shell, modeled as a coherent state of quantum matter. Without adding any physics beyond semiclassical gravity, we show that the universal gravitational coupling between matter and geometry induces an interaction between the shell and the negative-frequency modes present in the $|in \rangle$ vacuum state. We find that this interaction occurs unavoidably immediately after a trapping horizon forms, making it a universal feature of semiclassical gravitational collapse. The resulting transfer of energy from the collapsing matter to outgoing Hawking radiation acts as a \textit{gravitational quantum friction}, opposing the formation of trapped regions while simultaneously imprinting the information carried by the collapsing matter onto the emitted quanta. We discuss the implications of this mechanism for the black hole information-loss problem and speculate about the possible endpoints of collapse, potentially preventing horizon and singularity formation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用物理上合理的各向异性流体模型,围绕球对称黑洞构建物质配置,系统分析有效势垒中次级凸起出现时对应的物质分布特性和流体动力学性质,特别是检查物质轮廓的奇异性和流体特征速度的行为。

Conclusion: 黑洞周围各向异性流体产生的有效势垒次级凸起对应的物质配置是非物理的,因为它们表现出奇异的物质分布或发散的特征速度。这一结果对理解黑洞准正则模不稳定性和回声现象的物理可行性具有重要意义,表明某些理论预测的次级凸起效应可能缺乏物理基础。

Abstract: Matter fields localized around black holes can produce small secondary bumps in the effective potentials governing black-hole perturbations, potentially leading to quasinormal-mode spectral instabilities and late-time echoes. Considering physically motivated models of anisotropic fluids surrounding a spherically symmetric black hole, we show that the appearance of such a secondary bump is accompanied by either singular matter profiles or divergent characteristic speeds in the fluid. These features indicate that the corresponding matter configurations are unphysical. We further demonstrate that a secondary bump in the effective potential does not necessarily imply the existence of additional light rings in the spacetime.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用自力有效场论（Self-Force EFT）框架，将其中一个无旋体设置为无质量高能体以产生引力冲击波（GSW）背景，另一个体作为扰动源（满足能量比条件E₂/E₁≪1或m₂/E₁≪1）。选择特定参考系使EFT展现最大效力，将自力展开推进到1SF（一阶自力）阶次。推导冲击波背景上的精确引力子传播子，利用它计算动量空间中1SF阶的在壳波形。特别关注无质量-无质量碰撞情形，将波形计算扩展到后闵可夫斯基展开的所有阶，并分析其在超普朗克区域的重求和形式。

Conclusion: 本研究成功将自力有效场论应用于引力冲击波背景下的高能碰撞问题，通过选择适当参考系并推进到1SF阶，获得了在牛顿常数所有阶有效的解析结果。关键成果包括精确引力子传播子的计算、全阶后闵可夫斯基波形的获得，以及辐射能量角谱紫外有限性的证明。这些结果不仅验证了理论框架的一致性，也为理解超普朗克能标下的引力散射提供了重要的理论工具，对引力波物理和量子引力研究具有重要意义。

Abstract: We investigate the high-energy collision of two spinless bodies within the Self-Force Effective Field Theory, taking one of the two bodies to be massless and highly energetic, such that it generates a gravitational shockwave (GSW) background. The GSW is perturbed by a second body, either massless or massive, satisfying respectively $E_2/E_1\ll1$ or $m_2/E_1\ll1$. Although the Self-Force expansion for shockwaves is frame-dependent, we choose a frame in which this EFT manifests its full power, obtaining results valid to all orders in Newton's constant that make the underlying resummation patterns manifest. Specifically, we push the expansion to 1SF order and conjecture the all-order vanishing of the so-called recoil operators in a given gauge. We obtain the exact graviton propagator on the shockwave background, matching previous results, and use it to compute the on-shell waveform at 1SF order in momentum space, specialising to the massless-massless collision. The waveform is computed to all orders in the Post-Minkowskian expansion, and its resummed form proved to vanish at the leading order in the super-Planckian regime. We use this result to prove the UV finiteness of the angular spectrum of radiated energy in the region collinear to the shockwave with energy $E_1$.

</details>

Result: 数值计算结果确认在理论保持无病态的整个物理可允许参数空间范围内，不存在指数增长模式，即系统保持稳定。详细的准正则模谱分析揭示了高阶相互作用引入的若干显著特征，这些特征可作为区别于标准广义相对论的可观测信号。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了在Horndeski框架下包含四导数项的带电黑洞理论在物理参数空间内是稳定的，不会出现指数不稳定性。高阶修正对准正则模谱产生了可识别的特征性影响，为未来通过引力波观测等手段检验修正引力理论提供了理论预言和判据。

Abstract: We study linear gravitational perturbations and the corresponding quasinormal-mode spectra of electrically and magnetically charged black holes in the presence of higher-order corrections to the Einstein-Hilbert action. In particular, we consider a four-derivative interaction that couples the Riemann tensor to the electromagnetic field strength in the unique (Horndeski) combination that preserves second-order equations of motion. We provide a comprehensive analysis of the stability conditions. We numerically confirm the absence of exponentially growing modes throughout the physically admissible region of parameter space where the theory remains free of pathologies. We then investigate in detail the properties of the quasinormal-mode spectra and identify several distinctive features induced by the higher-order interaction.

</details>

Result: 证明了在给定无量纲电荷参数 Q̄ 下,Reissner-Nordström 黑洞-大质量标量场系统存在准共振(quasi-resonances)现象,即具有任意长寿命的线性化扰动模式。通过解析计算,确定了表征这些长寿命共振的黑洞-场临界质量谱 {Mμ_crit(Q̄)},该谱描述了系统准共振模式与黑洞电荷参数之间的定量关系。

Abstract: The physical and mathematical properties of the composed Reissner-Nordström-black-hole-massive-scalar-field system are studied {\it analytically} in the dimensionless large-mass $Mμ\gg1$ regime [here $\{M,μ\}$ are respectively the mass of the central black hole and the proper mass of the scalar field]. It is proved that, for a given value ${\bar Q}\equiv Q/M$ of the dimensionless charge parameter of the central black hole, the system is characterized by the presence of quasi-resonances, linearized perturbation modes with arbitrarily long lifetimes. In particular, using analytical techniques, we determine the black-hole-field critical mass spectrum $\{Mμ_{\text{crit}}({\bar Q})\}$ which characterizes the long-lived resonances of the composed physical system.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受近期突破性天基牛顿引力常数测量的启发,研究者希望设计一种更优化的空间测量方案,充分利用太空近乎无扰动的环境优势,结合先进的测量技术和统计分析方法,以提高引力常数G的测量精度。

Method: 该方案采用双通道测量架构:(1)第一通道基于静态源质量引力场中的近似恒定加速度测量;(2)第二通道采用锁相技术,利用测试质量和源质量的振荡坐标进行测量。采用协方差和互协方差方法对两个独立实验通道的数据进行优化统计分析。结合零坐标系技术以减少系统误差。对源质量的最优尺度和现有空间发射系统的运载能力进行了任务参数分析,并确定地球静止轨道为候选的低扰动测量位置。

Result: 通过任务参数分析,确定了源质量的最佳尺度范围,验证了方案与现有航天发射系统运载能力的兼容性。识别出地球静止轨道作为实现大幅减少扰动的候选位置,为实际任务实施提供了具体的轨道方案。

Conclusion: 提出的优化天基测量方案通过整合无扰动空间环境、双通道测量架构、锁相技术和协方差统计方法,为高精度测量牛顿引力常数提供了可行的技术路径。地球静止轨道的选择和对现有发射能力的兼容性分析表明该方案具有工程实现的可行性,有望显著提升引力常数的测量精度。

Abstract: Inspired by a recent ground-breaking space-based measurement of Newton's gravitational constant, we outline a proposal for a general layout of an optimized space-based approach that combines the advantages of a largely disturbance-free environment with lock-in techniques, null coordinates and the method of covariances. The method of covariances, and cross-covariances, is used for an optimized statistical analysis of data originating from two distinct experimental channels. One of two distinct measurement channels relies on an approximately constant acceleration in the gravitational field of static sources masses, while the second channel involves a lock-in measurement based on test and source masses with oscillating coordinates. Conceivable mission parameters are analyzed in terms of the optimum scale for the source masses and launch capabilities of currently available space launch systems. A candidate location for a largely disturbance-free position in geostationary orbit is identified.

</details>


### [103] [Shadows and gravitational perturbations of black bounces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09487)
*Alana C. L. Santos,Leandro A. Lessa,Roberto V. Maluf,Gonzalo J. Olmo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义相对论中由各向异性流体支持的黑反弹几何中的轴向引力扰动和阴影形成，分析了对称和非对称两类解的准正规模、有效势垒、引力波回声以及光子环特征，发现有视界的构型与标准黑洞几乎无法区分，而无视界对称构型可产生引力波回声和多个光子环，无视界非对称构型则接近Reissner-Nordström黑洞行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑反弹几何作为黑洞的正则化替代方案，可以避免时空奇点并提供从黑洞到虫洞的平滑过渡。本文旨在系统研究由各向异性流体支持的黑反弹几何的引力扰动性质和光学特征，探讨这些非奇异时空结构在引力波和电磁观测中的可观测特征，特别是区分它们与标准黑洞的可能性。

Method: 本文采用线性扰动理论分析轴向引力扰动，计算有效势垒和准正规模频率；通过数值求解波动方程研究引力波回声现象；从光学角度分析零测地线方程，计算光子球和阴影轮廓；检验准正规模与圆形零测地线不稳定性之间的eikonal对应关系。研究对象包括对称的Simpson-Visser型黑反弹和非对称的Reissner-Nordström度规形变两类解，系统比较有视界和无视界构型的差异。

Result: 有视界构型(对称或非对称)表现出单势垒有效势和与标准黑洞几乎无法区分的准正规频率；无视界对称构型可发展出多重势垒，产生振幅和间隔依赖于反弹参数a和密度参数ρ₀的引力波回声；无视界非对称解具有正则化有效势，不产生回声且与Reissner-Nordström情形相近。光学方面，有视界构型呈现单个光子球和与黑洞难以区分的阴影轮廓；无视界对称构型可支持多个光子环；无视界非对称构型可能支持单个光子环或不存在光子环。

Conclusion: 黑反弹几何的可观测特征强烈依赖于是否存在视界以及几何对称性。有视界的黑反弹构型在引力波和电磁观测中与标准黑洞几乎不可区分，标准的eikonal对应关系仍然成立。无视界构型则展现出独特的多重势垒和引力波回声现象(对称情形)或正则化行为(非对称情形)，多重势垒的出现阻碍了eikonal关系的直接推广，但波动与光学部门之间的强相似性表明可能存在有意义的现象学对应关系。这些结果为通过引力波回声和光学观测区分黑洞与某些非奇异时空提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate axial gravitational perturbations and shadow formation in black-bounce geometries supported by anisotropic fluids within general relativity. Two distinct classes of solutions are analyzed: a symmetric Simpson--Visser-like black bounce and an asymmetric deformation of the Reissner--Nordström metric, which can feature either a bounded interior region or an unbounded wormhole-like extension. Our findings indicate that configurations with horizons, whether symmetric or asymmetric, exhibit single-barrier effective potentials and quasinormal frequencies nearly indistinguishable from those of standard black holes. In contrast, horizonless symmetric configurations may develop multiple potential barriers, giving rise to gravitational-wave echoes whose amplitude and separation depend sensitively on the bounce parameter $a$ and the density parameter $ρ_0$. Horizonless asymmetric solutions, on the other hand, exhibit regularized effective potentials that do not produce echoes and remain phenomenologically close to the Reissner--Nordström case. From the optical perspective, both classes of solutions with horizons exhibit a single relevant photon sphere and shadow profiles nearly indistinguishable from those of their black-hole counterparts, while horizonless symmetric configurations can sustain multiple photon rings. Horizonless asymmetric configurations may instead support either a single photon ring or none. Finally, we recover the standard eikonal correspondence between quasinormal modes and the instability of circular null geodesics for configurations with horizons. For horizonless geometries, however, the emergence of multiple potential barriers prevents a straightforward extension of this relation, although the strong similarity between the wave and optical sectors suggests that a meaningful phenomenological correspondence may still persist.

</details>


### [104] [From driven oscillations to free ringdown: a particle plunging into Kerr](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09531)
*Colin Weller,Andrew Laeuger,Fulin Li,Neil Lu,Rowina S. Nathan,Sizheng Ma,Yanbei Chen,Ling Sun*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了克尔黑洞的准正则模式(QNM)响应，证明了环绕波形可以表现为常系数QNM叠加，但其各个QNM极点贡献实际上仍受源驱动。通过构造克尔格林函数的有理逼近，揭示了表观自由QNM叠加可在各模式动态解耦之前就已出现的物理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为准正则模式(QNM)叠加表示系统已脱离源驱动进入自由振荡阶段，但这一理解可能不完整。需要从第一性原理出发，深入研究粒子坠入克尔黑洞过程中QNM各极点贡献的演化机制，理解波形中QNM表示与源驱动之间的动力学关系，特别是在晚期时间波形如何从驱动态过渡到表观自由振荡态。

Method: 从QNM极点、物理留数和视界频率零点出发，构造克尔格林函数的第一性原理有理逼近。研究自旋参数为0.5-0.9的克尔黑洞中，从最内稳定圆轨道(INNERMOST stable circular orbit, ISCO)坠入的粒子系统。分析各个QNM极点贡献随时间的演化：追踪它们如何初始跟随源的瞬时复频率，以及何时从源解耦。重点研究晚期源趋近于第三及更高视界频率的阻尼振荡时，格林函数中的零点如何抵消源频率成分，而持续驱动如何在QNM频率产生振荡。

Result: 对于自旋0.5-0.9的克尔黑洞，从ISCO坠入的粒子产生的波形显示：每个QNM极点贡献初始时跟随源的瞬时复频率，仅在源衰减快于相应模式后才解耦。晚期时，源趋近于第三及更高视界频率的阻尼振荡之和。格林函数中对应的零点在相干波形中抵消了这些源频率成分，而持续的驱动在QNM频率产生振荡。因此，表观自由的QNM叠加可以在其各个贡献动力学解耦之前就已出现。

Conclusion: 环绕波形可以呈现常系数QNM表示形式，但这并不意味着各个QNM极点贡献已经脱离源驱动。通过克尔格林函数的有理逼近分析揭示：表观自由QNM叠加的出现是格林函数零点抵消源频率和持续驱动共同作用的结果，而非真正的动力学解耦。这一发现深化了对黑洞环绕信号物理本质的理解，对引力波数据分析中QNM提取和解释具有重要意义。

Abstract: We show that a ringdown waveform can admit a constant-coefficient quasinormal-mode (QNM) representation while its individual QNM-pole contributions remain driven. To establish this result, we construct a first-principles rational approximation to the Kerr Green's function from its QNM poles, physical residues, and horizon-frequency zeros. For particles plunging from the innermost stable circular orbit into Kerr black holes with spins 0.5-0.9, each QNM-pole contribution initially follows the source's instantaneous complex frequency and decouples only after the source decays faster than the corresponding mode. At late times, the source approaches a sum of damped oscillations at the third and higher horizon frequencies. The corresponding zeros in the Green's function cancel these source-frequency components in the coherent waveform, while the continuing drive generates oscillations at QNM frequencies. Thus, an apparently free QNM superposition can emerge before its individual contributions dynamically decouple from the source.

</details>


### [105] [Probing quantum chaos near a wormhole throat with a circular string](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09613)
*Ai-chen Li,Xin-Fei Li,Xuanting Ji*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了圆形探测弦的量子涨落在穿越虫洞喉部时是否会产生量子混沌响应，通过构建非时序关联函数(OTOC)分析了Ellis-Bronnikov虫洞和全局单极扩展中的指数增长行为，发现探测能量、喉部尺度和固体角亏损等参数对量子混沌特征有显著影响，并提供了一个可迁移的非全息框架用于在弯曲时空中直接诊断量子探测的量子混沌性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典圆弦嵌入在虫洞中虽然是周期性且径向稳定的,但其横向极化模式会经历时间依赖的潮汐势。研究者希望探究这些量子涨落是否会在穿越虫洞喉部时表现出量子混沌特性,以及如何通过非时序关联函数(OTOC)这一量子混沌诊断工具来刻画弯曲时空中量子探测的动力学敏感性。这为在非全息框架下研究引力系统的量子混沌提供了新途径。

Method: 1) 将世界面作用量展开至二次阶,对圆形探测弦的两个物理横向极化模式进行正则量子化;2) 利用不等时对易子构建非时序关联函数(OTOC)振幅;3) 对Ellis-Bronnikov虫洞和全局单极扩展虫洞几何进行数值分析;4) 研究OTOC的指数增长行为及其对探测能量、喉部尺度、固体角亏损等参数的依赖关系;5) 基于协变世界面涨落和实时对易子构建可迁移的理论框架,该框架不依赖于特定虫洞几何。

Result: 在Ellis-Bronnikov虫洞中,两个极化模式在首次穿越喉部时都表现出有限时间区间内的近似指数OTOC增长;相应的无量纲增长率(相对于物理时间测量)在研究参数范围内为正值,且通常随着探测能量相对于喉部尺度的增加而减小。在全局单极扩展中,增加固体角亏损会缩窄局部可放大模式的频带并抑制提取的增长率;足够强的缺陷可以几乎淬灭径向信号,而当能量-喉部尺度比较小时,角向通道保留了依赖于极化的非单调结构。

Conclusion: 研究表明圆形探测弦在穿越虫洞时确实会产生量子混沌响应特征,这些特征可通过OTOC的指数增长行为来刻画。重要的是,这些量度表征的是高斯涨落扇区中的有限时间动力学敏感性,不应将其等同于渐近多体混沌或热力学相变。该研究构建的框架仅依赖于协变世界面涨落和实时对易子,因此提供了一个可迁移的非全息方法,可直接应用量子混沌诊断工具于弯曲时空中的量子探测,为研究引力系统的量子特性开辟了新方向。

Abstract: We investigate whether quantum fluctuations of a circular probe string develop a quantum-chaotic response while traversing a wormhole throat. The classical circular-string embedding is periodic and radially stable, but its two physical transverse polarizations experience time-dependent tidal potentials. Expanding the world-sheet action to quadratic order, we canonically quantize these modes and construct out-of-time-ordered correlator(OTOC) amplitudes from their unequal-time commutators. For the Ellis--Bronnikov wormhole, both polarizations exhibit finite intervals of approximately exponential OTOC growth associated with the first throat passage. The corresponding dimensionless rate measured with respect to physical time is positive over the parameter range studied and generally decreases as the probe energy is increased relative to the throat scale. In the global-monopole extension, increasing the solid-angle deficit narrows the band of locally amplifiable modes and suppresses the extracted rates; a sufficiently strong defect can nearly quench the radial signal, while the angular channel retains a polarization-dependent non-monotonic structure when the energy-to-throat-scale ratio is small. These quantities characterize finite-time dynamical sensitivity in the Gaussian fluctuation sector and should not be identified with asymptotic many-body chaos or a thermodynamic phase transition. Although the numerical analysis uses two representative wormhole geometries, the construction depends only on covariant world-sheet fluctuations and real-time commutators. It therefore provides a transferable, non-holographic framework for applying quantum-chaos diagnostics directly to quantum probes in curved spacetimes.

</details>


### [106] [Ovcharenko-Podolsky Einstein-Maxwell gravitational instantons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09694)
*Hari K. Kunduri*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个显式的三参数环面Einstein-Maxwell引力瞬子族，这些完备黎曼流形具有消失的标量曲率并满足黎曼Einstein-Maxwell方程，通过对Ovcharenko-Podolsky构造的局部洛伦兹黑洞几何的解析延拓得到具有两个渐近端的整体度量结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找满足Einstein-Maxwell方程的完备引力瞬子解是引力理论和量子场论中的重要问题。本文旨在构造一类新的具有明确参数化形式的环面Einstein-Maxwell引力瞬子，这些解具有非平凡的拓扑结构（带有bolt的两个渐近端），可为理解引力瞬子的几何性质和黑洞解的欧几里得对应提供新视角。

Method: 本文采用解析延拓方法，从Ovcharenko-Podolsky构造的局部洛伦兹黑洞几何族出发，通过适当的解析延拓将其扩展为完备的黎曼度量。具体方法包括：(1) 构造显式的三参数环面度量族；(2) 验证该度量满足标量曲率为零的Einstein-Maxwell方程；(3) 分析度量的整体结构，证明其可扩展为具有两个渐近端的完备流形；(4) 确认渐近端微分同胚于$\mathbb{H}^2 \times S^2$，并在内部包含非塌缩的二维循环（bolt）。

Result: 成功构造了显式的三参数环面Einstein-Maxwell引力瞬子族，这些完备黎曼流形满足标量曲率消失且符合黎曼Einstein-Maxwell方程。证明了该度量可以扩展为具有特定拓扑结构的空间：两个渐近端微分同胚于$\mathbb{H}^2 \times S^2$，在内部存在非塌缩的二维bolt。该结果提供了从洛伦兹黑洞几何到欧几里得引力瞬子的明确联系。

Conclusion: 本文通过解析延拓方法，从Ovcharenko-Podolsky的局部洛伦兹黑洞几何出发，成功构造了一个新的三参数环面Einstein-Maxwell引力瞬子族。这些引力瞬子具有明确的几何和拓扑性质：完备性、标量曲率消失、两个$\mathbb{H}^2 \times S^2$型渐近端以及内部的非塌缩bolt结构。该结果丰富了引力瞬子解的分类，为研究Einstein-Maxwell理论的经典解和量子性质提供了新的数学工具。

Abstract: We present an explicit three-parameter family of toric Einstein-Maxwell gravitational instantons. These are complete Riemannian manifolds with vanishing scalar curvature which satisfy the Riemannian Einstein-Maxwell equations. We argue that the metric can be extended to a space with two asymptotic ends diffeomorphic to $\mathbb{H}^2 \times S^2$ with a non-collapsing two-cycle (bolt) in the bulk. The global metric is produced by suitably extending an analytic continuation of a local family of Lorentzian black hole geometries constructed by Ovcharenko-Podolsky.

</details>


### [107] [Analytic motions of spinning particles in Schwarzschild-(anti-)de Sitter spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09755)
*Rui-Hui Li,Xing-You Zhou,Chao-Jun Feng*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon形式框架下研究了带宇宙常数的静态球对称时空中自旋测试粒子的运动，通过对自旋变形五次多项式根结构的完整分类，揭示了Schwarzschild-de Sitter几何中自旋粒子的允许轨道类型，并发现在超过Stuchlík极限时自旋粒子仍可保持束缚态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自旋对粒子在带宇宙常数的弯曲时空中轨道运动的影响，特别是探索在无自旋粒子无法形成束缚轨道的极端条件下（超过Stuchlík极限），自旋效应是否能够改变粒子的动力学行为并允许束缚轨道存在。

Method: 采用Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon形式主义，在粒子自旋的线性近似下处理问题；利用背景时空的对称性，将径向和纬向动力学约化为由自旋变形五次多项式控制的一阶方程；对该五次多项式的实根结构进行完整分类；使用Lauricella超几何函数分段表示径向运动，五次多项式的正实根作为分支切割点；对纬向运动进行赤道面附近的小振荡分析。

Result: 完成了自旋变形五次多项式实根结构的完整分类，由此直接导出Schwarzschild-反de Sitter和Schwarzschild-de Sitter几何中自旋粒子的所有允许轨道类型；分析了根结构和稳定圆轨道区间对参数的依赖关系；发现当宇宙常数超过Stuchlík极限（此时无自旋粒子不存在束缚轨道）时，自旋粒子仍然可以保持束缚状态；获得了径向运动的Lauricella超几何函数表示和纬向运动的解析表达式。

Conclusion: 自旋效应显著改变了带宇宙常数时空中粒子的轨道动力学。通过对控制动力学的五次多项式根结构的系统分类，完整刻画了自旋粒子在Schwarzschild-(反)de Sitter时空中的可能运动模式。最重要的发现是，即使在超过Stuchlík极限、无自旋粒子无法形成束缚轨道的极端情况下，自旋粒子仍可维持束缚运动，这揭示了自旋作为稳定机制的关键作用，为理解宇宙学背景下的相对论性自旋动力学提供了完整的数学描述。

Abstract: The motion of a spinning test particle in a static, spherically symmetric spacetime with a cosmological constant is studied within the Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon formalism, working to linear order in the particle's spin. By exploiting the symmetries of the background, the radial and latitudinal dynamics are reduced to first-order equations governed by a spin-deformed quintic polynomial. A complete classification of the real root structure of this quintic is carried out, from which the allowed orbital types for spinning particles in the Schwarzschild-anti-de Sitter and Schwarzschild-de Sitter geometries follow directly. The parameter dependence of the root structure and of the stable circular orbit interval is analyzed. In particular, for values of the cosmological constant beyond the Stuchlík limit, where spinless particles admit no bound orbits, spinning particles can still remain bound. The radial motion is expressed piecewise in terms of Lauricella hypergeometric functions, with the simple positive real roots of the quintic acting as branch cuts. The latitudinal motion consists of small oscillations about an equatorial plane, and its phase, governed by the same quintic, admits an analogous analytic representation.

</details>


### [108] [Thermodynamics of Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09816)
*Zelin Zhang,Zhenyu Zhang,Bin Chen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson黑洞的热力学分析扩展到一般的Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson族，通过协变表面荷和正则可积方法确定了总角动量和正则质量，得到了与Kerr-Newman黑洞相同形式的第一定律和Smarr公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅针对中性和特殊带电的Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson黑洞进行热力学分析，需要将其扩展到更一般的Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson族（其中电荷和外场参数是独立的），以建立完整的热力学理论框架。

Method: 采用协变表面荷（covariant surface charges）和正则可积性（canonical integrability）方法。首先从引力场和电磁场的组合表面荷解析推导总角动量；然后通过对坐标时间平移相关的无穷小荷施加正则可积性条件，并以零场边界条件下的Kerr-Newman质量为约束，确定更一般的对称生成元，从而得到Christodoulou-Ruffini质量和相关的热力学势。

Result: 成功确定了Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson黑洞族的总角动量和正则质量（Christodoulou-Ruffini质量）。推导出的第一定律和Smarr公式与标准Kerr-Newman黑洞情形具有相同的形式。先前研究的两种Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson特殊情况可作为该一般结果的极限情况恢复。

Conclusion: 通过协变表面荷和正则可积性分析，本研究建立了一般Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson黑洞族的完整热力学框架。Christodoulou-Ruffini质量是满足正则可积性条件的正确质量定义，所得热力学第一定律和Smarr公式保持了与经典Kerr-Newman黑洞一致的形式，统一了之前的特殊情况，为带外场黑洞的热力学理论提供了系统性解决方案。

Abstract: In this work, we extend the thermodynamic analyses of the neutral and specially charged Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson black holes to the general Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson family, in which the electric and external-field parameters are independent. Using covariant surface charges and canonical integrability methods, we determine the total angular momentum and the canonical mass. The angular momentum follows analytically from the combined gravitational and electromagnetic surface charges. However, the infinitesimal charge associated with coordinate time translations is not integrable in solution space, so the mass must be associated with a more general symmetry generator. Imposing the canonical integrability conditions on this generator, together with the Kerr-Newman mass as the zero-field boundary condition, selects the Christodoulou-Ruffini mass and determines the associated thermodynamic potentials. The first law and Smarr formula take the same form as the ones in usual Kerr-Newman case, and the two previously studied Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson cases are recovered as special limits.

</details>


### [109] [Clock-noise propagation and calibration for phase-locking configurations in space-based gravitational-wave detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09820)
*Pan-Pan Wang,Cheng-Gang Shao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文针对空间引力波探测器中的主从相位锁定配置,推导了时钟噪声传播和校准的解析公式,提出了有序边带校准规则,并通过数值模拟验证了相位锁定实现可在最终校准前降低时钟噪声残差,而主激光相位噪声的消除仍依赖时延干涉测量(TDI)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于六个独立单向测量的时延干涉测量(TDI)公式未明确描述主从相位锁定配置中的共参考时钟结构。星载超稳振荡器的波动会将时钟噪声引入外差相位测量,需要在TDI中进行额外校准。因此需要针对主从相位锁定配置直接建立时钟噪声传播和校准的数学表述。

Method: 从载波和边带读出信号出发,推导时钟噪声耦合关系;构建保留非对易延迟的有序边带校准规则,该规则适用于满足传播和时钟系数闭合条件的双分支TDI组合;采用冻结等臂模型,针对具有相同分数频率噪声谱的相互独立星载时钟,比较有无相位锁定的匹配实现;对两个代表性的16链路组合进行数值模拟以验证解析谱和预测结果。

Result: 在所采用的冻结等臂模型中,针对0.1-10 mHz频段,所考虑的45个第二代组合(最多包含16个链路)在相位锁定实现中,最终时钟校准前的时钟噪声残差更小。两个代表性16链路组合的数值模拟重现了解析谱,验证了预测的噪声降低和校准效果。

Conclusion: 相位锁定实现可在最终校准前降低时钟噪声贡献,但主激光相位噪声的消除仍然依赖于时延干涉测量(TDI)技术。研究为空间引力波探测器中主从相位锁定配置下的时钟噪声处理提供了明确的理论框架和校准方法。

Abstract: In space-based gravitational-wave detectors, laser phase locking transfers the phase of weak received light to a local laser and maintains the interspacecraft heterodyne frequencies within the phasemeter bandwidth. Fluctuations of the onboard ultrastable oscillators introduce clock noise into these heterodyne phase measurements, requiring additional calibration in time-delay interferometry (TDI). Existing formulations based on six independently formed one-way measurements do not explicitly describe the common-reference clock structure of a master--slave phase-locking configuration. In this work, we formulate clock-noise propagation and calibration directly for this configuration. Starting from carrier and sideband readouts, we derive the clock-noise couplings and construct an ordered sideband-calibration rule. The rule retains noncommuting delays and applies to two-branch TDI combinations satisfying propagation and clock-coefficient closure. For mutually independent onboard clocks with identical fractional-frequency noise spectra, we compare matched realizations with and without phase locking. In the adopted frozen equal-arm model, 45 second-generation combinations with up to 16 links considered here have smaller clock-noise residuals in the phase-locking realization throughout 0.1--10~mHz, before final clock calibration. Numerical simulations of two representative 16-link combinations reproduce the analytical spectra and verify the predicted reduction and calibration. These results show that the phase-locking realizations reduce the clock-noise contribution before final calibration, while the cancellation of master-laser phase noise still relies on TDI.

</details>


### [110] [Prospects of electromagnetic follow-up of neutron star-black hole mergers in the LIGO-India era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09926)
*Yogita Kumari,Kanchan Soni,Sanjit Mitra*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了LIGO-India时代利用Vera C. Rubin天文台探测中子星-黑洞(NSBH)并合千新星对应体的前景。研究表明，添加LIGO-India探测器可使电磁后续观测数量增加约一倍，通过优化曝光时间策略，LHVA网络每年可探测到约4.19次(乐观)或0.79次(保守)引力波+电磁信号事件，为测量哈勃常数和探测哈勃参数提供重要数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星-黑洞并合是有前景的多信使源，但只有特定参数组合(质量比、黑洞自旋、中子星物态方程)的系统才能产生可探测的电磁对应体。随着LIGO-India的加入和Vera C. Rubin天文台的投入使用，需要评估在下一代探测网络中探测NSBH并合千新星对应体的能力，以优化观测策略并预测探测率，从而为哈勃常数测量和宇宙学研究提供支持。

Method: 研究采用以下方法：(1)模拟基于GW230529事件的NSBH星族，考虑两种有效潮汐形变参数范围(乐观和保守场景);(2)估算LHV(LIGO双站+Virgo)和LHVA(增加LIGO-India)探测器网络的引力波探测情况;(3)利用Rubin天文台模拟千新星发射的可探测性;(4)分析固定曝光时间策略下的电磁探测率;(5)提出基于预期对应体亮度和天区定位面积的事件特定曝光时间优化策略;(6)计算不同网络配置和星族模型下的年度引力波+电磁联合探测率。

Result: 研究结果显示：(1)添加LIGO-India使电磁后续观测数量约增加一倍，主要通过提高工作周期和减小天区定位面积(中位数)实现，并显著增加大距离处的探测数;(2)固定曝光策略下，电磁探测率在几百秒曝光时间时达到饱和，反映深度与天区覆盖的权衡;(3)采用优化曝光时间策略后，LHVA网络每年预期NSBH引力波+电磁探测率为：乐观星族4.19(-3.58,+9.66)次，保守星族0.79(-0.68,+1.83)次;相比之下，LHV网络分别为1.46(-1.25,+3.37)次和0.27(-0.23,+0.62)次;(4)所需Rubin后续观测时间在预期的目标机会观测分配范围内;(5)部分探测事件的红移范围约为0.1-0.2。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在未来十年内，可能会进行数次NSBH并合的引力波+电磁联合观测，其中部分事件处于红移0.1-0.2范围。LIGO-India的加入显著提升了探测能力，使年度联合探测率提高约2-3倍。基于事件特定参数的曝光时间优化策略能够有效平衡观测深度和天区覆盖。这些联合探测不仅对测量哈勃常数具有重要价值，还能帮助探测哈勃参数，为多信使天文学和宇宙学研究提供关键数据。

Abstract: Neutron star-black hole (NSBH) mergers are promising multimessenger sources, but only a subset is expected to produce detectable electromagnetic (EM) counterparts, depending on the binary mass ratio, black hole (BH) spin, and neutron star (NS) equation of state (EoS). We investigate the prospects for detecting kilonova counterparts to NSBH mergers in the LIGO-India era using the Vera C. Rubin Observatory (Rubin). We simulate GW230529-motivated NSBH populations with two effective tidal deformability ranges, estimate gravitational-wave detections for the LHV (LIGO-Livingston, LIGO-Hanford, Virgo) and LHVA (LHV with LIGO-India at Aundha) detector networks, and model the detectability of the associated kilonova emission with Rubin. We find that adding LIGO-India approximately doubles the number of EM follow-ups, primarily by increasing the duty cycle and reducing the median sky-localization area, and notably increases the number of such detections at large distances. For fixed-exposure strategies, the EM detection rate saturates at exposure times of a few hundred seconds, reflecting the trade-off between depth and sky coverage. We therefore introduce an event-specific exposure-time optimization strategy based on the expected counterpart brightness and sky-localization area. With this strategy, the expected NSBH GW+EM detection rate per year is 4.19(-3.58,+9.66) for the optimistic and 0.79(-0.68,+1.83) for the conservative population with the LHVA network, compared to 1.46(-1.25,+3.37) and 0.27(-0.23,+0.62), respectively, for LHV. The required Rubin follow-up time remains within the expected Target-of-Opportunity allocation. These results show that in the next decade, a few such NSBH follow-ups may take place, with a fraction of them in the redshift range of about 0.1-0.2, which will not only be useful for measuring the Hubble constant but can also help us in probing the Hubble parameter.

</details>


### [111] [Revealing the Quantum Signature of Gravity via Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09931)
*Partha Nandi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个统一的理论框架，结合时空几何、量子信息和引力波物理，证明了量子化引力波能够诱导探测器间的纠缠和非经典关联，而经典引力波背景则不能，从而为通过传播引力波探测量子引力提供了可操作的判据和几何-量子信息理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波能否作为探测引力量子本质的可操作探针是一个关键问题。现有研究缺乏统一的理论框架来直接比较经典和量子引力波在同一物理框架下的行为差异，需要建立能够将时空几何、量子信息和引力波物理相结合的系统性方法，以识别传播引力波的量子特征。

Method: 从线性化广义相对论中的测地线偏离方程出发，直接从时空几何导出有效探测器哈密顿量；构建探测器子系统与经典和量子化传播引力波场相互作用的完整量子动力学；基于几何和量子信息理论建立统一框架，使得能够在同一物理体系内直接比较经典和量子引力辐射描述；分析探测器状态的混合度、纠缠、量子相干、量子记忆和非经典关联等量子信息指标。

Result: 研究表明经典引力波背景只能在探测器状态中诱导混合度，但无法在探测器子系统之间产生真正的量子关联；量子化引力波则能够相干地介导引力诱导的纠缠、量子相干、量子记忆和非经典关联，提供了传播引力辐射量子本质的清晰可操作特征；介观量子力学振子为实验探测这些效应提供了有前景的途径。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了基于几何和量子信息的理论框架，用于通过传播引力波探索量子引力。量子化引力波与经典引力波在探测器关联效应上存在本质区别：量子引力波能够产生纠缠和非经典关联，而经典引力波不能。这为实验验证引力的量子本质提供了明确的可操作判据，并指出介观量子振子实验是实现这一目标的可行路径。

Abstract: Can propagating gravitational waves serve as operational probes of the quantum nature of gravity? We address this question by developing a unified theoretical framework that combines spacetime geometry, quantum information, and gravitational-wave physics. Starting from the geodesic deviation equation in linearized General Relativity, we derive the effective detector Hamiltonian directly from spacetime geometry and construct the complete quantum dynamics for detector subsystems interacting with both classical and quantized propagating gravitational-wave fields. This unified formulation enables a direct comparison between classical and quantum descriptions of gravitational radiation within the same physical framework. We demonstrate that classical gravitational-wave backgrounds can induce mixedness in the detector state but cannot generate genuine quantum correlations between the detector subsystems. In contrast, quantized gravitational waves coherently mediate gravity-induced entanglement, quantum coherence, quantum memory, and nonclassical correlations, providing clear operational signatures of the quantum nature of propagating gravitational radiation. We further discuss how mesoscopic quantum mechanical oscillators offer a promising route towards experimentally probing these effects. Our results establish a geometric and quantum-information-based framework for exploring quantum gravity through propagating gravitational waves.

</details>


### [112] [Critical Scalarization for a Self-Gravitating Bosonic Condensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09991)
*Meng-Yun Lai,Hyat Huang,Wen-Cong Gan,De-Cheng Zou,Yun Soo Myung*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在非相对论性自引力玻色凝聚体中建立了标量化的最小实现模型,揭示了相对论致密天体与非相对论凝聚态系统中标量化现象的共同机制,并指出在相干介质中实现实验室类比的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量化现象广泛存在于相对论致密天体中,但其物理机制复杂且难以在实验室中观测。研究者希望在非相对论框架下构建一个最小模型,以揭示标量化的核心物理机制,并探索在相干介质等可控系统中实现实验室类比的可能性,从而为理解和验证这一现象提供新途径。

Method: 采用自引力玻色凝聚体耦合标量响应场的理论模型。通过引入局域有效质量位移和非线性饱和效应,构建多分支结构并分析两种不同的相变路径。在线性稳定区域研究有限扰动驱动的一阶相变,分析阈值附近的I型对数标度行为;在线性不稳定区域研究小扰动的快子式增长动力学,探讨响应时间对增长速率和呼吸动力学的控制作用。

Result: 模型成功展现了多分支结构和两种相变路径。在线性稳定区,有限扰动可触发一阶相变,在阈值附近呈现I型对数标度特征。在线性不稳定区,小扰动呈现快子式(tachyonic)增长,响应时间同时控制增长过程和随后的呼吸振荡动力学。这些结果确认了相对论致密天体和非相对论凝聚态中标量化机制的共性。

Conclusion: 研究成功建立了标量化现象的最小非相对论实现,揭示了相对论致密天体与非相对论凝聚态系统中标量化的统一物理机制。该模型通过有效质量位移和非线性饱和这两个关键要素,再现了标量化的核心特征,包括多分支结构、一阶相变和快子不稳定性。这一工作为在相干介质等可控实验室系统中观测和研究标量化现象开辟了新的可能性,有望促进引力物理与凝聚态物理的交叉研究。

Abstract: We establish a minimal nonrelativistic realization of scalarization in a self-gravitating bosonic condensate coupled to a scalar response field. A local effective-mass shift and nonlinear saturation generate a multibranch structure with two distinct transition routes. In the linearly stable regime, a finite perturbation drives a first-order transition with type-I logarithmic scaling near threshold. Beyond the linear onset, small perturbations grow tachyonically, and the response time controls both the growth and the subsequent breathing dynamics. These results identify a common mechanism for scalarization across relativistic compact objects and nonrelativistic condensates, and point toward laboratory analogues in coherent media.

</details>


### [113] [Black hole and wormhole branches in gravitational decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10102)
*Francisco Tello-Ortiz,Y. Gomez-Leyton,Vitalii Vertogradov,Jean Baez Cuevas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了最小几何形变(MGD)应用于静态Schwarzschild黑洞的效应,发现当形变函数产生的最外根穿越种子视界时,系统会在临界耦合处分裂为两个互斥分支:一个保持黑洞结构,另一个形成双端虫洞,两者对应不同的非微分同胚流形,可通过第二同调群明确区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的最小几何形变方法应用于黑洞时,人们对耦合强度参数k如何影响时空的全局拓扑结构和几何完备性缺乏深入理解。特别是当形变函数h(r)产生的根r*与种子视界相互作用时,可能导致度规签名的改变和时空完备化的分歧,这一现象需要系统的数学刻画和物理解释。

Method: 采用最小几何形变(MGD)方法对静态Schwarzschild黑洞进行形变,通过求解θ扇区场方程和状态方程获得解耦函数h(r),构造形变度规F(r;k)=1+k·h(r)。系统分析形变函数最外根r*的位置与种子视界2M的关系,利用引理1证明当r*>2M时Lorentz签名在区间(2M,r*)内丢失,进而通过切除该区间并沿最小球面r=r*镜像对称延拓构造虫洞完备化。计算两种完备化对应的第二相对/绝对同调群H₂作为拓扑不变量。

Result: 证明了形变系统在临界耦合k_c处存在分支现象:k<k_c时保持黑洞视界结构,外部相对于视界的相对同调群H₂(Σ_BH,H)=0;k>k_c时最外根r*位于外部区域,度规在(2M,r*)失去Lorentz签名,无法光滑延拓通过种子视界,唯一可容许的Lorentz完备化是双端虫洞,其第二同调群H₂(Σ_WH)≅Z。两个分支对应不同的非微分同胚流形,由度规本身唯一确定。

Conclusion: 最小几何形变应用于Schwarzschild黑洞时,单一解耦函数h(r)可通过耦合参数k的取值产生两类完全不同的时空结构:黑洞与虫洞,两者在临界耦合处分离且互不兼容。度规签名的分析和第二同调群的计算提供了严格的数学判据来区分这两个分支,表明它们是不同的微分流形而非同一时空的拓扑变化。这一结果揭示了MGD方法中耦合参数的深层几何意义及其对全局时空结构的决定性影响。

Abstract: Minimal Geometric Deformation (MGD) applied to a static Schwarzschild black hole seed generates a single decoupler function $h(r)$, obtained by solving the $θ$-sector field equations together with an equation of state. Once $h(r)$ is fixed, the resulting one-parameter family is controlled by the coupling strength $k$ through $F(r;k)=1+k\,h(r)$. We show that, whenever the deformation develops a simple outermost root that crosses the seed horizon, the same fixed decoupler leads to two mutually exclusive branches associated with different global completions: on one side of the critical coupling the deformed metric preserves the seed horizon as a black hole, whereas on the other side the root $r_*>2M$ lies in the exterior and cannot be interpreted as an interior modification of the black hole geometry. We prove that this root forces a loss of Lorentzian signature on the interval $(2M,r_*)$, so that no smooth extension of the exterior metric through the seed horizon $r=2M$ exists once $r_*$ lies outside it. Within the static, spherically symmetric class considered here, the corresponding smooth Lorentzian completion is a two-ended wormhole obtained by excising $(2M,r_*)$ and doubling the region $r\geq r_*$ across the minimal sphere $\mathcal T=\{r=r_*\}$. No topology change of any single spacetime is claimed or required: $k>k_c$ and $k<k_c$ simply correspond to two different, non-diffeomorphic manifolds, and Lemma~1 below shows that the metric itself dictates which of the two is the admissible completion for a given $k$. We compute the second homology group of both completions explicitly, $H_2(Σ_{\rm BH},\mathcal H)=0$ for the black hole exterior relative to its horizon and $H_2(Σ_{\rm WH})\cong\mathbb Z$ for the completed wormhole manifold, giving a discrete invariant that distinguishes the two branches.

</details>


### [114] [Kaniadakis Holographic Dark Energy with Particle Horizon as IR Cutoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10320)
*A. Asvar,M. Mohammadi,A. Sheykhi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了基于Kaniadakis熵和粒子视界红外截断的全息暗能量模型,证明Kaniadakis形变能够在保留粒子视界的同时产生晚期宇宙加速膨胀所需的负压强,并系统分析了该模型的动力学演化、状态方程和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准爱因斯坦引力下,非相互作用的粒子视界全息暗能量无法产生晚期加速膨胀所需的足够负压强。研究者希望通过引入Kaniadakis熵形变来改变这一行为,同时保留粒子视界作为红外截断,从而构建能够解释宇宙加速膨胀的全息暗能量模型。

Method: 采用Kaniadakis熵构建全息暗能量模型,以粒子视界作为红外截断,同时一致性地修正全息密度和Friedmann背景方程。推导自治演化方程、有效暗能量状态方程和减速参数。利用绝热声速平方诊断有效流体稳定性,使用statefinder变量刻画与ΛCDM模型的偏离。研究了非相互作用情形以及包含相互作用强度b²=0.03和0.06的情形,并验证K→0极限下模型退化为标准理论。

Result: 对于代表性参数选择K=1.90×10⁻³⁶、ΩDE0=0.7和c²=0.64,非相互作用情形下减速参数在红移z≃0.585处改变符号,实现从减速到加速的转变。引入相互作用强度b²=0.03和0.06后,转变红移仅轻微改变为z≃0.586和0.589。这表明在所考虑的参数集下,相互作用并非产生加速的原因,加速转变已存在于非相互作用Kaniadakis模型中,相互作用仅对转变时间产生小幅偏移。验证了K→0极限连续恢复标准爱因斯坦引力粒子视界全息暗能量,其非相互作用模型保持减速状态。

Conclusion: Kaniadakis熵形变成功改变了粒子视界全息暗能量的动力学行为,使其能够在非相互作用情形下就产生晚期宇宙加速膨胀。加速转变主要源于Kaniadakis形变本身而非暗物质-暗能量相互作用,相互作用仅对转变时刻产生次要影响。该模型通过稳定性检验和statefinder分析,在K→0极限下正确退化为标准理论,为解释宇宙加速膨胀提供了基于修正熵的新视角。

Abstract: We construct a holographic dark-energy model using Kaniadakis entropy with the particle horizon as the infrared cutoff, consistently modifying both the holographic density and the Friedmann background. In standard Einstein gravity, noninteracting particle-horizon holographic dark energy (HDE) does not produce the sufficiently negative pressure required for late-time accelerated expansion. We show that the Kaniadakis deformation changes this behavior while the particle horizon is retained. For the representative parameter choice $K=1.90\times10^{-36}$, with $Ω_{\rm DE0}=0.7$ and $c^2=0.64$, the deceleration parameter changes sign at $z\simeq0.585$ in the noninteracting case. Including the interaction strengths $b^2=0.03$ and $0.06$ changes the transition only slightly, giving $z\simeq0.586$ and $0.589$, respectively. Thus, for the parameter set considered here, the interaction does not generate the acceleration; rather, the transition is already present in the noninteracting Kaniadakis model and the interaction produces only a small shift in its timing. We derive the autonomous evolution equations, the effective dark-energy equation of state, and the deceleration parameter. The adiabatic squared sound speed is also examined as a diagnostic of the effective-fluid stability, while statefinder variables are used to characterize deviations from $Λ$CDM. Finally, we verify that the $K\to0$ limit continuously recovers standard Einstein-gravity particle-horizon HDE, for which the noninteracting model remains decelerating.

</details>


### [115] [A Differentiable Parametric Model of Binary-Black-Hole Initial Data: I. Conformally flat Bowen-York punctures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10173)
*Frederik De Ceuster,Tjonnie G. -F. Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 提出了一种参数化、可微分的双黑洞初始数据模型，结合稀疏网格谱插值和牛顿迭代精化方法，能够高效求解非线性椭圆边值问题并提供解析导数，用于梯度优化和参数敏感性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双黑洞约束满足初始数据需要在整个物理参数空间中反复求解非线性椭圆边值问题，现有方法计算成本高且缺乏可微性。需要一种既能保证约束精度又能提供解析导数的高效参数化模型，以支持梯度优化、参数定位和敏感性分析等应用。

Method: 采用稀疏网格谱插值与牛顿精化相结合的混合策略：插值仅用于为椭圆求解器提供热启动初值，而非直接替代求解器；通过少量牛顿迭代将插值解精化至离散约束残差‖R‖∞≤10^(-10)，确保每个初始数据集都满足离散化约束。基于JAX实现以提供关于物理参数的解析导数，构建四维自旋对齐和八维一般自旋模型，用于准圆轨道双黑洞初始数据（采用共形平坦Bowen-York穿刺方法）。

Result: 成功构建了四维自旋对齐和八维一般自旋的双黑洞初始数据模型，所有返回的初始数据集均满足10^(-10)的约束容差。通过与TwoPunctures代码的对比验证了方法的正确性。实现了对物理ADM质量和角动量的精确参数定位，并通过专用的分离-切向动量模型成功应用于有效势偏心率降低。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了高精度、可微分的双黑洞初始数据生成，兼顾了计算效率和约束满足精度。通过将插值作为热启动而非最终解，保证了严格的约束满足；基于JAX的解析导数能力使得梯度优化和敏感性分析成为可能，为双黑洞数值相对论研究中的参数探索、偏心率控制等应用提供了强大工具。

Abstract: Constraint-satisfying initial data for binary black holes require the repeated solution of a nonlinear elliptic boundary-value problem throughout the physical parameter space. We present a para\-metric, differentiable initial-data model that combines sparse-grid spectral interpolation with Newton refinement over a family of binary configurations. Interpolation is used only to provide warm starts for the underlying elliptic solver, rather than to replace it. A small number of Newton iterations refines each interpolated solution to a discrete, row-equilibrated constraint residual $\|R\|_\infty\le10^{-10}$, independent of the interpolation error, so that every returned initial-data set satisfies the discretized constraint to that tolerance. Our JAX implementation provides analytic derivatives with respect to the physical parameters, enabling gradient-based parameter targeting, optimization, and sensitivity analysis. We demonstrate the method for quasi-circular binary-black-hole initial data using conformally flat Bowen--York punctures by constructing four-dimensional aligned-spin and eight-dimensional general-spin models. The resulting initial data are validated against TwoPunctures, and the method is applied to certified targeting of the physical Arnowitt--Deser--Misner mass and angular momentum and to effective-potential eccentricity reduction using a dedicated separation--tangential-momentum model.

</details>


### [116] [Universal Secular External Leg Corrections for Gauge Independent Scalar Self-Mass on de Sitter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10365)
*D. Glavan,S. P. Miao,T. Prokopec,R. P. Woodard*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在de Sitter背景下，引力子修正对无质量最小耦合标量有效场方程的规范依赖性消除方法。通过组合多类图（类似于t道交换但不取渐近极限），成功消除了1-loop阶的规范依赖性，包括非定域长期规范依赖和定域长期规范依赖，最终导出完全规范独立的有效场方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在宇宙学背景下，引力子修正的有效场方程存在规范依赖性问题，特别是在de Sitter时空中的长期（secular）规范依赖性。传统散射振幅方法中的渐近极限在宇宙学中存在问题，需要发展新的方法来消除这些规范依赖性，从而获得物理上有意义的、规范独立的有效场方程。

Method: 采用图组合方法，类似于两个大质量粒子之间通过无质量标量的t道交换散射振幅计算，但不取渐近极限。在平坦空间背景下，除了基本交换图外，引入五类额外的图来消除引力子规范固定泛函的依赖性。将这些图推广到de Sitter背景，并额外添加两类新图和对外线波函数进行长期修正，以完全消除定域长期规范依赖性。

Result: 在de Sitter背景下，基本的图组合成功消除了最重要的"非定域"长期规范依赖性。通过添加两类额外的图并对外线波函数进行长期修正，进一步消除了与外线相关的"定域"长期规范依赖性。最终在1-loop阶得到了完全规范独立的有效场方程。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了在de Sitter背景下消除引力子修正中规范依赖性的完整方法。通过系统地包含所有必要的图贡献和外线波函数的长期修正，在1-loop阶导出了完全规范独立的有效场方程，为宇宙学中的量子引力修正提供了可靠的理论框架。

Abstract: This work concerns a procedure for removing gauge dependence from graviton corrections to the effective field equations for a massless, minimally coupled scalar on de Sitter background. The procedure involves combining diagrams in the same way as the scattering amplitude for the $t$-channel exchange of the massless scalar between two massive particles, but {\it without} taking the asymptotic limits which are problematic in cosmology. Implementing this at 1-loop on flat space background requires five classes of diagrams, in addition to the naive exchange, and the combination of those diagrams does eliminate dependence on the graviton gauge fixing functional. When those same diagrams are generalized to de Sitter background, their combination cancels the most important (``nonlocal'') secular gauge dependence, but it leaves (``local'') secular gauge dependence that is associated with external legs. Local secular gauge dependence can be removed by adding two additional classes of diagrams, and making secular corrections to the external wavefunctions. The purpose of this paper is to include these additional corrections on de Sitter background in order to derive fully gauge independent effective field equations at 1-loop order.

</details>


### [117] [Testing Source Dependence in Gravitational-Wave Tests of General Relativity with Mutual Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10220)
*Yi-Fan Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于互信息的非参数框架，用于检测引力波广义相对论检验中的偏差参数与源性质的依赖关系，并通过大质量引力子模拟验证了该方法，同时分析了GWTC-4.0等公开数据，发现偏差参数与有效自旋、源质量等存在相关性，为诊断广义相对论检验中的偏差起源提供了星表级分析工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波广义相对论检验中的现象学偏差参数可能不是普适的基本性质，而是依赖于源性质的导出量；波形系统误差或数据质量问题也可能导致表观违反，这些都可能与源性质相关。因此需要在星表层面识别系统性趋势，以诊断单个事件并理解偏差的物理起源。

Method: 引入基于互信息的非参数框架来检验偏差参数是否依赖于源性质（如质量、距离、自旋等）。首先使用大质量引力子波形模拟验证框架有效性，检验参数化后爱因斯坦系数与光度距离和源质量的依赖关系；然后应用于GWTC-4.0和4-OGC的真实数据，分析现象学参数化检验、修正色散检验和宇称破缺检验中的偏差参数与源性质的相关性。

Result: 模拟验证显示：在当前第四观测运行的探测器灵敏度下，当引力子质量≥1×10⁻²²eV时，质量和距离依赖性变得明显，而广义相对论对照组产生零结果。真实数据分析表明：现象学偏差参数与有效自旋存在初步相关性，修正色散或宇称破缺参数与源质量的相关性清晰可见。

Conclusion: 星表级依赖性分析能够识别系统性模式，可用于诊断广义相对论检验中偏差的起源。该框架有效补充了单事件分析和参数化分层星族检验方法，为区分真实的理论偏差与系统误差提供了新工具，有助于提高引力波天文学中基本物理检验的可靠性。

Abstract: Phenomenological deviation parameters used in gravitational-wave tests of general relativity need not represent universal fundamental properties of the sources. Instead, a deviation parameter can be a derived quantity that depends on source properties. Apparent violations of general relativity arising from waveform systematics or data-quality issues can also depend on source properties. In addition to diagnosing individual events, it is helpful to identify systematic trends at the population level. We introduce a nonparametric framework that uses mutual information to test whether the deviations depend on source properties. We validate the framework using simulations with massive graviton waveforms. Assuming we do not have prior knowledge of the underlying theory, massive graviton effects can be captured by a parameterized post-Einstein coefficient that is expected to depend on the luminosity distance and source mass. The results show that the mass and distance dependencies become clear for graviton masses of $\geq 1\times10^{-22}\,\mathrm{eV}$ at detector sensitivities representative of the current fourth observing run, while the controlled general relativity simulations yield null results. We then analyze public real-data products for tests of general relativity from GWTC-4.0 and 4-OGC, including phenomenological parameterized tests, modified dispersion tests, and parity violation tests. The phenomenological deviation parameters have tentative correlations with effective spin, and correlations of modified-dispersion or parity-violation parameters with source mass are visible. These results demonstrate that catalog-level dependence can identify patterns and thus be used to diagnose the origin of deviations in testing general relativity. The framework therefore complements single-event analyses and parameterized hierarchical population tests.

</details>


### [118] [Field quantization in rotating frames: coordinate covariance and the circular-detector response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10390)
*Sidney Natzuka Junior,Carlos Augusto Domingues Zarro,Matheus dos Santos Soares*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对相对论旋转参考系中的标量场量子化和探测器响应进行统一分析,比较了刚性旋转和Trocheris-Takeno(TT)旋转描述,证明了Bogoliubov混合消失、旋转真空态缺失与探测器激发三者之间的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在旋转参考系中的量子场论研究中,关于旋转坐标表示、旋转真空态选择以及旋转探测器响应这三个问题经常被混淆。需要澄清刚性旋转和TT旋转框架中标量场量子化的本质,特别是Bogoliubov系数、基态选择与探测器响应之间的关系,解决看似矛盾的现象:为何Bogoliubov混合为零但探测器仍有响应。

Method: 采用统一的场论分析框架,在无界Minkowski时空中明确区分三个核心问题:(i)固定量子态在旋转坐标系中的表示;(ii)旋转时间演化是否选择全局基态;(iii)旋转探测器对真空的响应。通过计算Bogoliubov系数、模式求和重构Wightman函数,分析刚性和TT时间平移生成元的性质。采用Unruh-DeWitt探测器模型,经过显式Hadamard减法计算探测器在Minkowski真空中的稳态响应,并将响应与负共转频率关联。

Result: 研究发现:(1)刚性和TT旋转模式的正频子空间与惯性模式相同,Bogoliubov beta系数为零,模式求和重现Minkowski Wightman函数;(2)两种旋转时间流都不选择全局标量基态,但原因不同——刚性生成元非全局类时且下方无界,而TT时间平移不是Killing对称;(3)圆周运动的Unruh-DeWitt探测器在Minkowski真空中具有稳态非零响应;(4)该响应与负共转频率相关且呈非热分布。

Conclusion: 证明了Bogoliubov混合消失、对称性选择的旋转真空态缺失以及探测器激发现象三者之间是相互一致的,不存在矛盾。旋转探测器的非零响应源于负共转频率贡献而非粒子产生,且该响应呈现非热特性。这一统一分析澄清了旋转参考系量子场论中长期存在的概念混淆,为理解非惯性系中的量子效应提供了清晰的理论框架。

Abstract: We provide a unified analysis of scalar-field quantization and detector response in relativistic rotating frames, comparing rigid and Trocheris-Takeno (TT) descriptions in unbounded Minkowski spacetime. We distinguish three questions that are often conflated: (i) how a fixed quantum state is represented in rotating coordinates, (ii) whether rotating time evolution selects a global ground state, and (iii) how a rotating detector responds to the vacuum. Coordinate-transformed rigid and TT modes span the same positive-frequency subspace as inertial modes: their Bogoliubov beta coefficients vanish and their mode sums reproduce the Minkowski Wightman function. Neither rotating time flow selects a global scalar ground state, although for different reasons: the rigid generator is not globally timelike and is unbounded below, whereas TT time translation is not a Killing symmetry. Nevertheless, a circular Unruh-DeWitt detector in the Minkowski vacuum has a stationary, nonzero response. We relate this response to negative corotating frequencies, evaluate it after an explicit Hadamard subtraction, and show that it is nonthermal. Thus vanishing Bogoliubov mixing, the absence of a symmetry-selected rotating vacuum, and detector excitation are mutually consistent.

</details>


### [119] [Pulsar Timing Response and Spatial Correlations of Shear Modes in Torsionless Palatini Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10251)
*Tian-Shi Li,Yu-Mei Wu,Chang Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Palatini时空中非度量性产生的剪切模式对脉冲星计时的影响,推导了单脉冲星红移和双点相关函数,发现各向同性随机剪切背景在短波长极限下产生纯偶极型角关联$Γ_{ab}^{\mathrm{sh}}(ζ)=\cosζ$,但该信号与太阳系历表误差存在空间简并性,需要更多信息来区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳赫兹波段的脉冲星计时残差空间关联是表征随机引力波背景和探测其偏振内容的关键观测量。在无挠Palatini时空中,非度量性可以产生两种额外的剪切模式(剪切-x和剪切-y模式)。本文旨在研究这些剪切模式如何通过脉冲星和地球的运动影响脉冲星计时观测,并探索在脉冲星计时阵列(PTA)数据中识别Palatini剪切特征的可能性。

Method: 假设电磁场与物理度规最小耦合,且地球和脉冲星具有非零的有效超动量响应,作者推导了:1)单脉冲星红移表达式;2)频域双点相关函数;3)相应的空间角关联函数。针对平稳且各向同性的随机剪切背景,在短波长极限下计算了不同脉冱星对之间的归一化重叠缩减函数(ORF),并与太阳系历表误差产生的信号进行了比较分析。

Result: 对于平稳各向同性的随机剪切背景,不同脉冲星之间的归一化重叠缩减函数在短波长极限下简化为纯偶极型形式$Γ_{ab}^{\mathrm{sh}}(ζ)=\cosζ$,其中$ζ$是两颗脉冲星之间的角间距。研究发现,各向同性太阳系历表误差也能产生相同的偶极型角关联,导致两种信号之间存在空间简并性,仅依靠角关联无法区分这两种物理起源。

Conclusion: Palatini时空中的剪切模式在脉冲星计时观测中产生特征性的偶极型空间关联($\cosζ$),但由于与太阳系历表误差的空间简并性,仅凭角关联函数不足以在PTA数据中明确识别Palatini剪切特征。这一简并性凸显了利用角关联之外的其他信息(如频谱特征、时间依赖性等)来区分和识别Palatini剪切信号的重要性,为未来PTA数据分析提供了重要的理论指导。

Abstract: In the nanohertz band, spatial correlations between pulsar timing residuals provide a key observable for characterizing stochastic gravitational-wave backgrounds and probing their polarization content. In torsionless Palatini spacetime, nonmetricity can generate two additional shear modes, referred to as the shear-$x$ and shear-$y$ modes. In this work, we investigate the pulsar timing response produced by the shear-induced motions of the emitting pulsar and the receiving Earth. Assuming that the electromagnetic field is minimally coupled to the physical metric and that the Earth and pulsar possess non-negligible effective hypermomentum responses, we derive the single-pulsar redshift, the frequency-domain two-point correlation function, and the corresponding spatial correlation. For a stationary and isotropic stochastic shear background, the normalized overlap reduction function (ORF) for distinct pulsars reduces in the short-wavelength limit to the pure dipolar form $Γ_{ab}^{\mathrm{sh}}(ζ)=\cosζ$, where $ζ$ is the angular separation between the two pulsars. The same dipolar correlation can also be produced by isotropic Solar System ephemeris errors, leading to a spatial degeneracy between the two signals. This degeneracy highlights the importance of information beyond the angular correlation for identifying Palatini shear signatures in PTA data.

</details>


### [120] [Point particles in general relativity: beyond linear perturbation theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10521)
*Jørgen Musaeus,Adam Pound,Samuel D. Upton*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在先前工作基础上,提出了广义相对论二阶微扰理论中点粒子的实用化"骨架化"场方程形式,简化了运动方程推导,并通过Hadamard正则化等方法避免奇点问题,为黑洞双星系统的二阶自力计算开辟了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点粒子在完全非线性广义相对论中通常定义不清,虽然在线性微扰理论中可以自然定义。作者之前的工作已在通用背景时空的二阶微扰理论中通过匹配渐近展开严格定义了点粒子,但需要更实用的场方程形式来进行实际计算,特别是需要解决奇点"punctures"问题并实现严格的正则化方法应用。

Method: 采用匹配渐近展开方法在二阶微扰理论框架下重新表述场方程,将其改写为更加"骨架化"(skeletonized)的实用形式。利用这种新形式推导运动方程,并采用Hadamard正则化等成熟的正则化技术替代奇异的"punctures"方法处理点粒子的奇点问题。

Result: 成功将二阶微扰场方程重新表述为更实用的骨架化形式,该形式能够简化运动方程的推导过程。证明了二阶微扰理论计算可以绕过奇异punctures的使用,并能严格应用Hadamard正则化等现成的正则化方法。

Conclusion: 本文建立的骨架化场方程框架和正则化方法为黑洞双星系统中的二阶自力(self-force)计算提供了新的技术路径,使得在广义相对论二阶微扰理论中对点粒子的处理更加严格和实用,有望推动引力波源模型的精确计算。

Abstract: Point particles are generically ill defined in fully nonlinear general relativity, although they are naturally well defined in linear perturbation theory. In a previous paper, two of us showed how they can be rigorously defined from matched asymptotic expansions at second order in perturbation theory on a generic background spacetime. Here we show how the resulting field equations can be recast in a more practical, more fully ``skeletonized'' form. We also show that this form of the field equations enables simple derivations of equations of motion. Finally, we show how calculations in second-order perturbation theory can bypass the use of singular ``punctures'' and make rigorous use of off-the-shelf regularization methods such as Hadamard regularization, opening new avenues to second-order self-force calculations in black hole binaries.

</details>


### [121] [Generalized Black holes with Fully Non-aligned Electromagnetic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10332)
*Hideo Furugori,Shinya Tomizawa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在四维Einstein-Maxwell理论中,通过Plebański-Demiański适配参数化发展了广义Ovcharenko-Podolský解,构建了具有非零非对齐电磁场的新型广义黑洞族。通过移除先前参数化中的限制,保留了独立电荷参数q,并建立了八参数族解,扩展了已有的七参数族,详细分析了其黑洞视界、加速视界、极端性等性质,并建立了与Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson族的显式对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的Ovcharenko-Podolský解存在参数化限制,该限制在场方程中并非必需。研究者希望通过移除这一限制,发展更一般的参数化方案,以构建更广泛的广义黑洞解族,特别是具有非零、完全非对齐电磁场的Petrov D型时空解,从而扩展四维Einstein-Maxwell理论中的黑洞解空间。

Method: 采用Plebański-Demiański适配的参数化方法处理Ovcharenko-Podolský解,在平移坐标系中移除先前不必要的限制,保留独立电荷参数q(可为实数或纯虚数)。施加Griffiths-Podolský形式的角条件以允许球形拓扑的Killing视界截面。对非扭曲族和l=0扭曲族给出显式度规和电磁场表达式,通过代数分析确定四个代数分支,并使用真正的Griffiths-Podolský形式建立与已知解族的显式对应关系。

Result: 成功构建了八参数类的广义黑洞解,扩展了Ovcharenko和Podolský的七参数类。对于q²≥0的情况,度规和电磁场均承认到带电Plebański-Demiański解的光滑对齐极限(Λ=0且e²+g²=q²)。确定了l=0扭曲族的有效无NUT分支及其退化情况。建立了非加速有效无NUT子类与Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson族的显式对应,并用新参数表达了电磁通量荷。q²<0扇区包含了最近识别的真正不带电的Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson解。

Conclusion: 通过移除先前参数化中的非必要限制,本研究成功发展了更一般的八参数广义黑洞解族,丰富了四维Einstein-Maxwell理论中具有非对齐电磁场的黑洞解的分类。研究详细分析了这些解的物理性质(视界结构、极端性、锥性等),建立了与经典黑洞解族的明确联系,并揭示了包含真正不带电Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson解在内的新物理扇区,为理解广义黑洞的几何和电磁结构提供了更完整的理论框架。

Abstract: We develop a Plebański--Demiański-adapted parametrization of the general Ovcharenko--Podolský solution and construct new families of generalized black holes in four-dimensional Einstein--Maxwell theory. These Petrov type~D spacetimes possess non-null, fully non-aligned electromagnetic fields. A restriction imposed in the previous parametrization is not required by the field equations in shifted coordinates. Removing it retains an independent charge parameter $q$, which may be real or purely imaginary. For $q^2\geq0$, both the metric and electromagnetic field admit a smooth aligned limit to the charged Plebański--Demiański solution with $Λ=0$ and $e^2+g^2=q^2$. We impose angular conditions in the Griffiths--Podolský form allowing Killing horizon cross sections with spherical topology. The resulting eight-parameter class extends the seven-parameter class of Ovcharenko and Podolský [arXiv:2508.04850]. The remaining normalization condition is generically quartic, defining four algebraic branches. We give explicit metrics and electromagnetic fields for the non-twisting and $l=0$ twisting families and analyze their black hole and acceleration horizons, extremality, conicity, and further limits. We determine the effective-NUT-free branches of the latter family and their degenerate cases. Using the genuine Griffiths--Podolský form, we establish an explicit correspondence between the non-accelerating effective-NUT-free subclass and the Kerr--Newman--Bertotti--Robinson family and express its electromagnetic flux charges in our parameters. The $q^2<0$ sector includes the genuine uncharged Kerr--Bertotti--Robinson solution recently identified by Ovcharenko and Podolský [arXiv:2608.21672].

</details>


### [122] [Parity symmetry as a diagnostic for spin-precessing binary-black-hole waveform models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10345)
*Héctor Estellés,Sascha Husa,Eleanor Hamilton,Cecilio García-Quirós,Panagiota Kolitsidou,Joan Llobera-Querol*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义相对论的宇称不变性对双黑洞系统波形和残余性质的约束，发现多个主流波形模型存在宇称对称性违反，并通过修正恢复了对称性，为引力波波形模型提供了重要的一致性检验方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论的宇称不变性要求双黑洞系统在反转平面内自旋和观测方向后，波形和残余性质应满足精确的对称关系。然而，现有的自旋进动波形模型是否严格满足这一基本对称性尚未得到系统验证。验证和强制实施这种对称性对于确保波形模型的物理一致性和可靠性至关重要。

Method: 1) 基于宇称不变性推导出双黑洞波形和残余性质应满足的精确关系；2) 构建保守的、非最大化重叠度诊断工具作为一致性检验；3) 对七个先进的波形模型（IMRPhenomXO4a、IMRPhenomXPNR、NRSur7dq4、SEOBNRv5PHM等）进行宇称对称性测试；4) 追踪违反对称性的具体原因，包括相位锚点选择、参数化方案和校准系数；5) 提出局部修正方案并验证其有效性；6) 对GWTC-5.0中八个引力波事件进行重新分析以评估修正的天体物理影响。

Result: 基于对齐自旋校准和宇称协变进动动力学构建的模型满足数值精度的对称性。IMRPhenomXO4a和IMRPhenomXPNR显示中位宇称残差约1.6×10^-3且存在较宽的上尾；NRSur7dq4在高质量比和大自旋情况下违反最严重；SEOBNRv5PHM的不对称扩展表现出较小的局部违反；NRSur7dq4Remnant在反冲中的残差大于最终质量和自旋。违反原因分别归因于IMRPhenom反对称模式的观测者依赖相位锚点、NRSur7dq4代理拟合的非不变参数化，以及SEOBNRv5PHM不对称扩展中一个非不变的数值相对论校准系数。局部修正使IMRPhenom和SEOBNRv5PHM恢复到数值精度的宇称对称性。对八个GWTC-5.0事件的重新分析显示天体物理解释无定性变化，但IMRPhenom修正在个别边缘化后验中产生最高5×10^-2的Jensen-Shannon散度。

Conclusion: 精确对称性为波形模型提供了低成本、模型独立的一致性检验方法，应在校准波形模型的构建中通过设计强制实施宇称不变性。研究证实了对称性检验能够识别现有模型中的系统性缺陷，局部修正可以有效恢复对称性而不显著改变天体物理推断。这为未来引力波波形模型的开发和验证提供了重要的理论指导和实践标准。

Abstract: Parity invariance of general relativity imposes exact relations between waveforms and remnant properties of binary-black-hole configurations related by reversal of the in-plane spins and observer direction. We summarize these relations and construct consistency tests for spin-precessing waveform and remnant models. Applying a conservative, unmaximized overlap diagnostic to seven state-of-the-art waveform models, we find that models built from aligned-spin calibrated waveforms and parity-covariant precession dynamics satisfy the symmetry to numerical precision. The original \texttt{IMRPhenomXO4a} and \texttt{IMRPhenomXPNR} implementations instead exhibit median parity residuals of approximately $1.6\times10^{-3}$ with broad upper tails, while \texttt{NRSur7dq4} shows its largest violations at high mass ratio and large spin magnitude. The asymmetric extension of \texttt{SEOBNRv5PHM} exhibits a smaller, localized violation, while \texttt{NRSur7dq4Remnant} shows larger residuals in the recoil than in the final mass and spin. We trace these violations to an observer-dependent phase anchor in the \texttt{IMRPhenom} antisymmetric mode, non-invariant parameterization of the \texttt{NRSur7dq4} surrogate fits, and one non-invariant NR-calibrated coefficient in the asymmetric \texttt{SEOBNRv5PHM} extension. Local corrections restore waveform parity to numerical precision for the \texttt{IMRPhenom} and \texttt{SEOBNRv5PHM} cases; restoring it in \texttt{NRSur7dq4} requires refitting the surrogate. Reanalyses of eight GWTC-5.0 events show no qualitative change in astrophysical interpretation, although the \texttt{IMRPhenom} correction yields Jensen-Shannon divergences up to $5\times10^{-2}$ in individual marginalized posteriors. Exact symmetries thus provide inexpensive, model-independent consistency tests and should be imposed by construction in calibrated waveform models.

</details>


### [123] [A Novel Scheme for Inter-Satellite Integrated Laser Ranging and Communication in Space-Based GW Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10354)
*Zihao Shao,Siyuan Xie,Kai Luo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型星间集成激光链路方案，通过将数据编码的伪随机噪声(PRN)信号先调制到超稳振荡器(USO)时钟信号上再调制到激光载波上，显著抑制了空间引力波探测系统中激光相位测量噪声，同时满足时钟噪声传输要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于空间的引力波探测系统中，采用伪随机噪声(PRN)码进行星间测距时，当前集成方案下数据编码的PRN信号会在激光相位测量中引入过大的噪声。为了抑制这种噪声影响，需要提出一种改进的星间激光链路集成方案。

Method: 提出了一种星间集成激光链路新方案：将用于星间绝对距离测量和通信的数据编码PRN信号，首先相位调制到超稳振荡器(USO)时钟信号上，然后再将调制后的时钟信号相位调制到激光载波上。通过改变调制顺序和信号处理流程，实现对激光相位测量噪声的抑制。

Result: 理论分析和仿真结果表明：与当前采用的方案相比，所提出的新方案显著抑制了激光相位测量噪声；同时，数据编码PRN信号对时钟噪声传输产生的影响被限制在系统要求范围内，满足空间引力波探测的性能指标。

Conclusion: 提出的星间集成激光链路方案通过优化调制架构，有效解决了空间引力波探测系统中PRN信号引入的激光相位测量噪声问题。该方案在保证星间测距、通信和时钟传输功能的同时，显著提升了系统的噪声性能，为空间引力波探测提供了更优的技术解决方案。

Abstract: Adopting pseudo-random noise (PRN) codes for inter-satellite ranging, the resulting data-encoded PRN signal induces excessive laser phase measurement noise under the currently adopted integrated scheme in space-based gravitational wave (GW) detection systems. To suppress the noise, a scheme of inter-satellite integrated laser link is presented in this Letter, in which the data-encoded PRN signal for inter-satellite absolute distance measurement and communication is phase-modulated onto the ultra-stable oscillator (USO) clock signal before phase modulation onto the laser carrier. Theoretical analysis and simulation results show that in the proposed scheme the noise is significantly suppressed compared with the currently-adopted one, while the noise stemming from the data-encoded PRN signal on clock noise transfer is limited within the requirement.

</details>
